# Weak/strong-balance experiment: complete score tables



This appendix reports every threshold and lead separately for all 12 scored contexts. ORIGINAL is the paired baseline. The three prior cases are context references; only the eight new frozen-plan cases enter qualification. No scores are averaged across thresholds, leads, or cases.



All hourly amounts are differences between consecutive cumulative WRF precipitation histories, scored at the common 3,688-point RN1 mask. CSI = H/(H+M+FA); FAR = FA/(H+FA). H, M, and FA denote hits, misses, and false alarms. The table retains undefined ratios as `—`.
